In this work, we conduct a comparative analysis of two automatic label error detection methods - Confident Learning and Dataset Cartography - on three Russian text classification corpora of varying size, number of classes, and domain: ru_emotion_e-culture (49,123 examples, emotion classification), RuCoLA (8,524 examples, linguistic acceptability), and TERRa (2,337 examples, textual entailment recognition). We use the pre-trained rubert-base-cased model fine-tuned on each corpus. To verify the meaningfulness of filtering, we conduct control experiments with random removal of an equivalent number of examples. Results show that the effectiveness of both methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics: on large corpora with low noise levels, filtering does not improve performance, while on small datasets with high noise, Confident Learning achieves a significant F1-macro improvement. Dataset Cartography demonstrates more conservative behavior, removing fewer examples. Across all corpora, targeted removal by both methods outperforms random removal, confirming the meaningfulness of the approaches.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29826)
*Leijiang Gu,Zhen Zeng,Feng Li,Xinjian Gao,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LDKE框架通过局部化关键层和输入解纠缠，解决了MKE中编辑不泛化和意外修改的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态知识编辑方法能修改目标事实对，但存在局限性：无法将编辑泛化到逻辑相关查询，且经常导致对视觉或语义相关但无关信息的意外修改

Method: 提出局部化和解纠缠知识编辑框架，包含快速局部化模块用于高效识别和更新关键层，以及解纠缠分类器用于适当路由输入以保护无关知识

Result: 在各种基准测试和MLLM上的实验表明，LDKE在将编辑传播到相关上下文的同时保持高局部性方面取得了优异性能

Conclusion: LDKE通过解决因果不对齐和特征纠缠问题，实现了精确且泛化的多模态知识编辑

Abstract: Existing methods in Multimodal Knowledge Editing (MKE) have advanced the ability to correct outdated or inaccurate knowledge in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, they exhibit a critical limitation: while effectively modifying target factual pairs, they fail to generalize edits to logically related queries and often cause unintended alterations to unrelated but visually or semantically linked information. We identify and formalize two underlying failure modes causing this issue: Causal Misalignment, which confines edits to the specific sample, and Feature Entanglement, which causes unintended alterations to coupled but irrelevant information. To address these issues, we propose Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing (LDKE), a new framework that achieves precise and generalized editing by localizing fact-specific model layers and disentangling target-relevant inputs from irrelevant ones. Our approach introduces a Fast Localization module to identify and update critical layers efficiently, along with a Disentanglement Classifier that routes inputs appropriately to preserve unrelated knowledge. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks and MLLMs demonstrate that LDKE achieves superior performance in propagating edits to related contexts while maintaining high locality.

</details>


### [186] [EvoRubric: Self-Evolving Rubric-Driven RL for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29847)
*Xin Guan,Xiaomeng Hu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Bo Zhang,Zijian Li,Pengjun Xie,Bo Liu,Jiuxin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoRubric：一个单策略协同进化的强化学习框架，通过统一回答生成和评分标准生成来消除对静态标准或外部评分标准生成器的依赖，解决了开放域生成中缺乏确定奖励的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在可验证领域中显著推进了大语言模型，但为开放域生成对齐模型仍然极具挑战性，因为缺乏明确的奖励。现有的基于评分标准的RL方法使用显式标准来缓解这一问题，但依赖于静态的人工标注评分标准导致策略滞后，或者依赖昂贵的外部专有模型进行动态更新。

Method: 提出EvoRubric框架，将回答生成和评分标准生成统一在单个参数化策略下，动态切换推理器和评分标准生成器两个角色。引入多级验证管道（包含元验证器、零方差剪枝和Leave-One-Out同行共识机制）来防止奖励攻击并确保生成信号的可靠性。验证后的标准动态存档到记忆池中，产生密集的多目标奖励来持续协同优化两个角色。

Result: 在医学、写作和科学领域的广泛实验表明，EvoRubric始终优于传统的静态和外部LLM驱动的对齐方法。值得注意的是，该框架与人类专家先验兼容。当用专家标注的评分标准初始化时，EvoRubric能进一步发现新的判别性维度，比仅依赖静态专家标注获得更好的性能。

Conclusion: EvoRubric是一个有效的协同进化RL框架，通过内部生成和验证评分标准解决了开放域对齐中缺乏明确奖励的问题，减少了对外部资源或静态标准的依赖，同时能利用专家知识进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has significantly advanced Large Language Models (LLMs) in verifiable domains, but aligning models for open-ended generation remains profoundly challenging due to the lack of definitive rewards. Current rubric-based RL methods mitigate this by employing explicit criteria; however, they rely heavily on static, human-annotated rubrics that inevitably cause policy lag, or expensive external proprietary models for dynamic updates. In this paper, we propose EvoRubric, a novel single-policy co-evolutionary RL framework that eliminates the reliance on static criteria and on external rubric generators. By unifying response generation and rubric generation under a single parameterized policy, EvoRubric dynamically alternates between a Reasoner and a Rubric Generator. To prevent reward hacking and ensure the reliability of generated signals, we introduce a multi-level verification pipeline featuring a meta-verifier, zero-variance pruning, and a Leave-One-Out peer consensus mechanism. Validated criteria are dynamically archived into a memory pool, yielding dense, multi-objective rewards to continuously co-optimize both roles. Extensive experiments across Medical, Writing, and Science domains demonstrate that EvoRubric consistently outperforms traditional static and external-LLM-driven alignment methods. Notably, our framework is compatible with human-expert priors. When initialized with expert-annotated rubrics, EvoRubric can further uncover novel, discriminative dimensions, achieving better performance than relying solely on static expert annotations.

</details>


### [187] [CRITIC-R1: Learning Structured Critics for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29886)
*Wenhan Xiao,Ziwei Zhang,Chuanyue Yu,Xingcheng Fu,Qingyun Sun,Runhua Xu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRITIC-R1是一个基于强化学习的结构化批评框架，通过明确错误诊断来改进检索增强生成(RAG)系统的性能，减少幻觉和推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法存在幻觉和细微推理错误问题，而现有的外部批评方法提供的是粗粒度、弱结构化的反馈，存在过度干预问题，导致噪声和不可靠的细化，限制了纠正效果。

Method: 提出结构化批评框架CRITIC-R1，将RAG批评制定为明确的错误诊断问题，使用强化学习训练。将常见RAG错误分类到多个诊断维度（判断、错误位置、推理分析、修复生成）。设计两种奖励函数：保守判断对齐(CJA)用于校准高层判断并缓解过度干预；诊断质量对齐(DQA)通过门控奖励改进细粒度诊断反馈。使用基于GRPO的强化学习训练批评模型，收集外部LLM教师模型的过程级监督数据。

Result: 在五个QA基准测试上的实验表明，CRITIC-R1在答案质量上一致性地优于强大的RAG基线方法。

Conclusion: CRITIC-R1通过结构化错误诊断和强化学习训练，有效地改进了RAG系统的性能，解决了现有批评方法在反馈结构和干预程度上的局限性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive question answering by incorporating external evidence. However, existing RAG methods still suffer from hallucinations and subtle reasoning errors. Recent studies introduce external critics to refine RAG outputs, yet they often provide coarse-grained and weakly structured feedback, exhibit over-aggressive intervention, and lead to noisy and unreliable refinement, limiting their effectiveness for correction. To tackle these issues, we propose CRITIC-R1, a structured critic framework that formulates and learns RAG critique as an explicit error diagnosis problem using reinforcement learning (RL). Our framework categorizes common RAG errors into multiple diagnostic dimensions, including verdict, error location, reasoning analysis, and fix generation. To learn these capabilities, we design two reward functions: Conservative Judgement Alignment (CJA) first encourages calibrated high-level judgements while mitigating the over-aggressive phenomenon, whereas Diagnostic Quality Alignment (DQA) further improves fine-grained diagnostic feedback through gated rewards. We train the critic model using GRPO-based RL with process-level supervision collected from external LLM teacher models. Experiments across five QA benchmarks show that CRITIC-R1 consistently improves answer quality over strong RAG baselines.
  Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/critic-r1-FCB0

</details>


### [188] [Internal Representation, Not Clinical Knowledge: Where Apparent LLM Triage Failures Originate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29889)
*David Fraile Navarro,Berardino Como,Jialei Sheng,Soundariya Ananthan,Shlomo Berkovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，临床分诊任务中，LLMs在多项选择题格式下表现不佳的问题根源在于输出格式而非临床表征能力缺失。当使用稀疏自编码器分析时，发现相同的医疗特征在两种输出格式下都激活，但在多项选择题的决策标记处这些特征变得沉默，决策由支架和格式特征驱动而非医疗特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究显示，患者在临床分诊任务中对LLMs的评估报告了高误判率，但同一病例在自由文本格式下得分不同。研究人员想要探究这种差异是否源于模型临床表征的改变，还是仅仅是从保存的表征到答案的映射发生了变化。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征分析Gemma 3 4B/12B IT和Qwen3-8B模型。采用三种独立方法：自然语言自编码器言语化、决策标记对数属性归因和顶特征表征。进行行为实验包括结构化与自然语言输入的对比、选项顺序随机化以排除位置偏倚。

Result: 发现相同的医疗特征在两种输出格式下都对共享的临床叙述激活，但在多项选择题的决策标记处这些特征在所有模型和所有病例中都变得沉默。支架和格式特征而非医疗特征驱动决策对数。多项选择题惩罚在结构和自然语言输入下都发生逆转，选项顺序随机化排除了位置偏倚，性能差距主要由"偏一"决策（模型选择与正确答案相邻的敏度字母）而非知识缺失造成。

Conclusion: 临床分诊任务中LLMs在多项选择题格式下的失败源于输出格式而非临床表征缺失。模型保留了医疗理解，但在格式约束下无法正确地将这种理解映射到选择题答案。

Abstract: Patient-voiced clinical-triage benchmarks report high under-triage rates for consumer LLMs for constrained multiple-choice output, yet the same cases score differently with free-text. We ask whether output format changes the model's \emph{clinical representation} or only the mapping from a preserved representation to an answer. Using sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features in Gemma 3 4B/12B IT and Qwen3-8B, we find the same medical features fire on the shared clinical narrative under both formats but go {silent} at the multiple-choice decision token in all the cases at every model. Three independent methods (natural-language autoencoder verbalization, decision-token logit attribution, and top-feature characterization) agree that scaffold and format features, but not medical features, drive the decision logits. Behaviorally, the multiple-choice penalty inverts under both structured and natural-language input, option-order shuffle rules out positional bias, and the gap is dominated by off-by-one decision (the model picks an adjacent acuity letter to the gold answer) rather than knowledge failure. Thus, the failure originates in the output format and not in the clinical representation.

</details>


### [189] [ExCAM: Explainable Cultural Awareness Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29897)
*Christoph Leiter,Haiyue Song,Hour Kaing,Jin Tei,Hideki Tanaka,Masao Utiyama,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ExCAM（可解释文化意识度量），这是第一个专门用于识别、评分和解释指令-输出对中文化错误的评估指标，并创建了ExCAM40k数据集用于训练和评估，在平衡测试集上达到80%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型的文化意识对于确保生成文本的公平性和跨文化应用泛化性至关重要。现有基准测试需要耗时昂贵的人工标注，且缺乏评估自由文本文化意识的现代方法。

Method: 1. 提出ExCAM度量标准：首个专门识别、评分和解释文化错误的评估指标；2. 构建ExCAM40k数据集：整合9个现有基准测试并重新格式化，通过合成错误增强；3. 与多个基线（包括GPT-5）进行比较评估。

Result: ExCAM在平衡测试集上达到最高的错误检测率，准确率高达80%，优于包括GPT-5在内的多个基线模型，实现了细粒度和可解释的文化评估。

Conclusion: ExCAM为自由文本的细粒度和可解释文化评估开辟了新途径，解决了现有基准测试在时间成本、评估机制过时和自由文本评估不足方面的问题。

Abstract: Evaluating the cultural awareness of large language models is crucial to ensure the fairness of generated text and the generalizability of applications across the world. Recent benchmarks explore cultural goods like food or values like behavior in stressful situations through the lens of question answering or text generation tasks. However, creating these benchmarks requires time-intensive and costly human annotations. Also, benchmarks that evaluate cultural awareness in free text are scarce and often rely on dated evaluation mechanisms. To address this gap, we introduce ExCAM, an Explainable Cultural Awareness Metric, which is, to our knowledge, the first dedicated evaluation metric that identifies, rates and explains cultural errors in instruction-output pairs. To train and evaluate ExCAM, we introduce ExCAM40k, a dataset comprised of nine existing benchmarks that we reformat and enhance with synthetic errors. Compared to several baselines, including GPT-5, ExCAM achieves the highest error detection rate with up to 80% accuracy on a balanced test set. Therefore, ExCAM opens the pathway towards fine-grained and explainable cultural evaluation of free text.

</details>


### [190] [Causal Interventions on Continuous Variables: A Case Study on Verb Bias in Steering Vectors for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29971)
*Zhenghao Herbert Zhou,R. Thomas McCoy,Robert Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种对语言模型表示中连续变量进行因果干预的方法，应用于动词偏好这一连续特征，证实该方法有效但连接到情境学习仍有挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型表示中的因果干预主要针对离散特征（如语法数），但语言模型也必须使用连续分级特征。需要开发对连续变量进行因果干预的方法，以验证这些特征的因果作用

Method: 提出连续变量因果干预方法：给定与分级目标变量配对的激活向量，定位该变量的低维方向，并沿此方向编辑向量以实现反事实目标值。应用于动词偏好特征，从大语言模型中提取控制向量

Result: 动词偏好在提取的控制向量中具有因果表示：反事实编辑能系统性地改变下游结构偏好。控制向量编码可能驱动情境学习中错误驱动更新行为的错误信号，但这些方面在下游生成中未被因果使用

Conclusion: 因果干预可以应用于连续变量，证实了动词偏好等连续特征的因果作用。然而，将连续变量与情境学习联系起来仍具挑战性，因为控制向量中的错误信号方面在下游生成中未被因果利用

Abstract: Causal interventions in language model representations have largely targeted discrete features, like grammatical number. However, language models must also make use of features that are graded. We introduce a method for causal intervention on continuous variables: given activation vectors paired with a graded target variable, we localize a low-dimensional direction for that variable and use this direction to edit a vectors toward counterfactual target values. We apply this method to a continuous feature that is well-studied in psycholinguistics, namely verb bias (which reflects which syntactic structures tend to follow a given verb). We show that verb bias is causally represented in steering vectors extracted from large language models: counterfactual edits to verb bias systematically shift downstream structural preferences. Verb bias has also previously been linked to in-context learning; in further analyses, we find that steering vectors encode error signals that could drive the error-driven update behavior seen in in-context learning but that these aspects of the steering vectors are not causally used in downstream production. Overall, these results show causal interventions can be applied to continuous variables, though connecting continuous variables to in-context learning remains a challenge.

</details>


### [191] [Adapting Multilingual Embedding Models to Turkish via Cross-Lingual Tokenizer Surgery and Offline Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29992)
*M. Ali Bayram,Banu Diri,Savaş Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了embeddingmagibu-200m，这是一个专门针对土耳其语的句子嵌入模型，采用高效的三阶段适应管道，避免了完整的预训练过程，以较低的成本实现了优于300M参数教师模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的土耳其语嵌入模型存在两个主要限制：1) 早期BERT-based土耳其语编码器仅支持512个token的上下文窗口，限制了长文本处理能力；2) 训练土耳其语嵌入模型通常需要完整的预训练，计算成本高昂。本文旨在开发一个更高效、支持更长上下文且性能优越的土耳其语句子嵌入模型。

Method: 提出了一个高效的三阶段适应管道：1) 构建土耳其语优化的多语言分词器：通过从教师模型词汇表中剪枝冗余标记，并基于40种语言语料库的频率分析整合多语言标记，创建131,072词汇表的分词器；2) 克隆教师嵌入模型：保持transformer骨干权重不变，通过均值组合标记映射为新词汇表初始化兼容的嵌入表；3) 离线嵌入蒸馏：使用预先计算的教师向量，在平衡的40语言Wikipedia语料库上采用余弦相似度目标进行蒸馏训练。

Result: 模型包含约200M参数，在STSbTR数据集上获得了77.55%/77.45%的Pearson/Spearman相关性，超越了300M参数的教师模型（73.84%/72.92%）。在TR-MTEB（26个任务）上获得了63.9%的平均分数（在26个模型中排名第7），比教师模型参数少33%的情况下提供了有竞争力的成本-质量权衡。训练仅需约4小时单GPU，总成本5-20美元。

Conclusion: embeddingmagibu-200m模型通过高效的适应管道实现了优于更大教师模型的性能，支持8,192个token的上下文窗口，显著扩展了土耳其语嵌入模型的能力。该方法避免了昂贵的完整预训练，为资源受限的语言提供了可行的嵌入模型开发方案。作者发布了所有相关资源以促进可复现性和下游应用。

Abstract: Sentence embeddings are a foundational component for semantic search, clustering, classification, and retrieval-augmented generation. This paper presents embeddingmagibu-200m, a Turkish-focused sentence embedding model that produces 768-dimensional L2-normalized vectors and supports an 8,192-token context window, far exceeding the 512-token limit of earlier BERT-based Turkish encoders. Instead of full pretraining, an efficient three-stage adaptation pipeline is introduced: (1) construct a Turkish-optimized multilingual tokenizer with a 131,072 vocabulary by pruning redundant tokens from the teacher's vocabulary and incorporating multilingual tokens via frequency analysis on a 40-language corpus, (2) clone a teacher embedding model while preserving transformer backbone weights and initializing a compatible embedding table for the new vocabulary via mean-composition token mapping, and (3) perform offline embedding distillation from precomputed teacher vectors using a cosine similarity objective over a balanced 40-language Wikipedia corpus. The resulting student model contains approximately 200M parameters and trains in roughly four hours on a single GPU by avoiding online teacher inference during training, at a total cost of $5-$20. Empirically, Pearson/Spearman correlations of 77.55%/77.45% are obtained on STSbTR, surpassing the 300M-parameter teacher model (73.84%/72.92%). On TR-MTEB (26 tasks), a mean score of 63.9% is achieved (7th out of 26 models), providing a competitive cost-quality trade-off with 33% fewer parameters than the teacher. To facilitate reproducibility and downstream use, all artifacts are released including model weights, tokenizer files, precomputed embedding datasets, and open-source cloning and distillation tooling.

</details>


### [192] [Latent Performance Profiling of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30018)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Ayan Sengupta,Suparna Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti,Amlan Chakrabarti,Supratik Chakraborty,Partha Pratim Das,Lipika Dey,Richa Singh,Mayank Vatsa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)框架，通过分析LLM的隐藏激活和输出分布进行任务无关的评估，超越传统基准测试的表面准确性评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估主要依赖标准化基准（如MMLU PRO、BBH、IFEval），存在数据污染、任务范围狭窄、与现实可靠性对齐弱等问题，只关注模型输出什么而非如何处理信息、校准不确定性或结构化内部知识

Method: 引入Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)框架，从隐藏激活和输出分布推导任务无关的诊断指标，定义一组关于模型潜在表示和动态的标量度量，揭示尺度无关的特征

Result: 在8个LLM（0.5B-14B）上进行实证分析，发现具有相似基准分数的模型可能展现不同的潜在特征（如熵或适应性差异），设计了与内在指标对齐的合成探针用于不确定性和符号推理评估

Conclusion: 建议在基准测试外同时报告LPP指标，提供更深入、可解释的模型行为理解，支持更可靠的模型选择、安全评估和超越表面准确性的评估

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently achieve impressive scores on standardized benchmarks, yet accuracy alone offers a limited view of their capabilities. Evaluating open-source LLMs through leaderboards faces persistent issues like data contamination, narrow task scope, and weak alignment with real-world reliability. Benchmark-based evaluations such as MMLU PRO, BBH, or IFEval primarily capture \textit{what} a model outputs on fixed test sets, not \textit{how} it processes information, calibrates uncertainty, or structures internal knowledge. In this article, we advocate for a shift from benchmark-centric evaluation toward a complementary, \textit{state-centered intrinsic assessment} of LLMs. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)} -- a framework that derives task-agnostic diagnostics from hidden activations and output distributions. LPP defines a set of scalar metrics on a model's latent representations and dynamics, revealing scale-independent traits that enable interpretable comparisons and uncover hidden vulnerabilities. Unlike static accuracy scores, LPP provides stable, architecture-sensitive signatures across models of similar size. With extensive empirical analyses across eight LLMs, spanning a size range of 0.5B-14B, we demonstrate that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit contrasting latent profiles, such as differences in entropy or adaptability. Guided by these insights, we design synthetic probes for uncertainty and symbolic reasoning that align with intrinsic metrics while decoupling from leaderboard bias. We recommend that reporting LPP alongside benchmarks provides a deeper, interpretable understanding of model behavior, enabling more reliable model selection, safety assessment, and evaluation beyond surface-level accuracy.

</details>


### [193] [Recovering Diversity Without Losing Alignment: A DPO Recipe for Post-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30021)
*Vinay Samuel,Yapei Chang,Mohit Iyyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REDIPO是一种离线DPO数据构建流程，用于在保持指令模型对齐优势的同时恢复多样化的有效答案模式，通过从基础模型和指令模型中采样响应、重写基础模型响应、过滤候选答案以及构建偏好对来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放性问题通常存在多个有效答案，但后训练往往将LLM的输出空间缩小到少数典型响应。需要一种方法在保持指令模型对齐优势的同时恢复多样化的有效答案模式。

Method: 对于每个提示，REDIPO从基础模型和指令模型中采样响应，使用指令模型重写基础模型响应，筛选候选答案的安全性和指令遵循质量，构建偏好对以在具有相似指令遵循奖励的候选答案中支持边际多样性的响应。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B、OLMo-3-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上，REDIPO分别将NoveltyBench distinct_k指标提升了134%、33%和44%，而DivPO的变化为0%、-6%和-4%。这些改进基本保持了MTBench、IFEval和Arena-Hard性能，并降低了HarmBench直接类别攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过精心构建的偏好数据可以重新引入来自基础模型生成的不同有效答案，同时保持后训练的对齐优势。边际多样性对选择和基础响应重写推动多样性提升，而过滤和质量边界配对有助于保持对齐。

Abstract: Many open-ended instructions have multiple valid answers that users can benefit from seeing, but post-training often narrows an LLM's output space toward a small set of canonical responses. We introduce REDIPO, an offline DPO data-construction pipeline for recovering distinct valid answer modes while preserving the alignment benefits of the instruct model. For each prompt, REDIPO samples responses from both base and instruct models, rewrites base-model responses with the instruct model, filters candidates for safety and instruction-following quality, and builds preference pairs that favor marginally diverse responses among candidates with similar instruction-following reward. Across Qwen3-4B, OLMo-3-7B, and LLaMA-3.1-8B, REDIPO improves NoveltyBench distinct_k by 134%, 33%, and 44% relative to the instruct checkpoints, while DivPO changes diversity by 0%, -6%, and -4% on the same models. These gains largely maintain MTBench, IFEval, and Arena-Hard performance, and reduce direct-category HarmBench attack success rate. Ablations show that marginal-diversity pair selection and base-response rewriting drive the diversity gains, while filtering and quality-bounded pairing help maintain alignment. Overall, our results show that diverse valid answers from base-model generations can be reintroduced through carefully constructed preference data while retaining the alignment benefits of post-training. We release our code and data at https://github.com/vsamuel2003/RiDiPO.

</details>


### [194] [Give it Space! Explicit Disentangling of Positional and Semantic Representations in Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30022)
*Pierre-Antoine Lequeu,Camille Barboule,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分离语义、绝对位置和相对位置流，揭示了Transformer中位置编码的内部机制，发现标准位置编码不能稳健保留宏观结构，而解耦方法能改善语言表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的位置编码方法（如RoPE）在长上下文理解和检索等任务上表现不佳，且位置信息在Transformer内部如何处理和存储仍不清晰。通过理解内部位置机制，有助于设计更好的位置编码方法。

Method: 修改编码器Transformer，使其处理三个显式解耦的流：语义流、绝对位置(AP)流和相对位置(RP)流，并将掩码语言建模(MLM)目标限制在语义流中。这种解耦使机制研究更清晰。

Result: 1) 独立的AP子空间自发坍缩为捕获文档结构的低频二维流形
2) 注意力头分化为结构和语义导向两组，RP专门支持后者
3) 标准位置编码不能稳健保留宏观结构：RoPE和RP仅弱编码，而纠缠的AP在MLM压力下在最后层丢失结构信息
解耦方法在Flash-Holmes基准测试的65个语言现象中的49个上改善了语言表示

Conclusion: 通过解耦语义、绝对位置和相对位置信号，可以更好地理解和保留位置信息，从而改进Transformer的位置编码机制，提升语言表示能力。这为设计更有效的位置编码方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Positional encoding (PE) underpins how permutation-invariant Transformers represent sequence order, yet how positional information is processed and stored remains poorly understood. Modern PE methods such as RoPE still struggle on tasks such as long-context understanding or retrieval \cite{chen-etal-2025-hope}. Hence, a better understanding of the internal positional mechanism could help design better PE. Building on evidence that positional and semantic signals occupy nearly orthogonal subspaces in trained Transformers, we modify an encoder Transformer to process three explicitly disentangled streams: semantic, absolute positional (AP) and relative positional (RP), and confine the masked-language-modeling (MLM) objective to the semantic stream. This decoupling enables a clean mechanistic study and yields three take-aways. (1) The isolated AP subspace spontaneously collapses into a low-frequency two-dimensional manifold that captures the structure of the document; (2) Attention heads specialize into structure and semantic-oriented groups, with RP exclusively supporting the latter; (3) Standard positional encodings do not robustly retain macroscopic structure: RoPE and RP only weakly encode it, and entangled AP loses it in the final layers under MLM pressure. The disentangled approach preserves positional encoding, which improves linguistic representation on 49 of the 65 linguistic phenomena of the Flash-Holmes probing benchmark.

</details>


### [195] [Who Am I? History-Aware Profiles for Student Simulation in Tutoring Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30051)
*Zhangqi Duan,Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Simon Woodhead,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种历史条件化学生模拟方法，使用强化学习框架来更准确地预测学生的对话回合，通过利用学生在数学学习平台上的历史数据来改进LLM驱动的自动辅导工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型驱动的自动辅导工具中的学生模拟大多局限于对话内模拟，缺乏基于学生历史知识和行为上下文的信息。这主要是因为现有方法没有充分地利用学生过去的答题历史或对话交互记录，导致模拟缺乏对个体学生特征的准确把握。

Method: 提出一个双组件框架：1) 档案生成器：总结学生的学习历史；2) 模拟器：基于生成的档案预测学生对话回合。两个组件都使用强化学习进行训练，以优化生成准确反映学生特征的档案。

Result: 该方法在一个首创的、来自数学学习平台的真实世界学生对话和答题数据集上进行了评估。大量实验表明，该方法显著优于基线方法，并证明了历史信息、学生档案和强化学习训练的重要性。

Conclusion: 历史条件化学生模拟是一个关键任务，通过将学生的学习历史纳入考虑，可以显著提高LLM驱动的自动辅导工具中学生模拟的准确性。提出的强化学习框架有效地利用了历史数据，为开发更精准的智能辅导系统提供了重要方法。

Abstract: A key part of developing large language model (LLM)-powered, automated tutoring tools is student simulation, i.e., using LLMs to role-play as students, which can facilitate tutor model evaluation and training. Existing work mostly focuses on within-dialogue simulation, which lacks context on student knowledge and behavior, partly due to not grounding in past student question-answering or dialogue interactions. In this work, we introduce the task of history-conditioned student simulation, where the goal is to accurately predict student dialogue turns by leveraging information in the student's learning history. We propose a two-component framework in which a profile generator summarizes a student's history and a simulator predicts student turns conditioned on the resulting profile. We train both components with reinforcement learning (RL), yielding profiles optimized for faithful student simulation. We evaluate our method and baselines on the first-of-its-kind real-world dataset of student dialogues and question responses that we collect from a math learning platform. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms baselines, and demonstrate the importance of history, profiles, and RL training.

</details>


### [196] [HEART-Bench: Do LLM Agents Exhibit Human-like Psychology?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30058)
*Weihan Peng,Chenxu Zhang,Qianao Wang,Yuling Shi,Heng Lian,Qihong Mao,Jiahao Pang,Chunliang Feng,Bowen Li,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个新颖的基准测试，用于系统评估LLM智能体是否能够模拟连贯的、类似人类的心理学特征，通过将正交的大五人格特质与结构化自传式记忆相结合，构建多样化的人类角色来进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM智能体已展现出卓越的任务导向能力（如规划、推理和行动），但很少有研究将其视为完整的人类人格，其中情感维度同等重要。现有研究缺乏系统评估LLM智能体模拟人类心理学能力的方法。

Method: 构建了基于正交大五人格特质的11个多样化人类角色，每个角色配置了1000个结构化自传式情景记忆，这些记忆分布在理论基础的发育生命阶段。设计了基于DIAMONDS分类法（包含责任、智力、逆境、交配、积极性、消极性、欺骗性和社交性八个维度）的64个决策场景。通过系统性人工验证和筛选，最终获得包含673个多项选择题的基准测试。

Result: 开发了一个系统化的基准测试框架，能够评估LLM智能体是否能整合其内在人格特质和自传记忆，做出与其特定心理特征一致的行为决策。该基准为研究基于LLM的智能体中类似人类的情感、人格一致性和价值观一致的行为决策提供了原则性和可扩展的测试平台。

Conclusion: 这项研究填补了LLM智能体在模拟人类心理学方面的评估空白，提出的基准测试为后续研究提供了一个标准化的工具，有助于推动LLM智能体在情感智能、人格一致性和行为决策方面的发展。

Abstract: While LLM agents have demonstrated remarkable task-oriented abilities such as planning, reasoning, and action, few works have treated them as complete human personalities where emotional dimensions hold equal importance. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark to systematically assess whether LLM agents can simulate coherent, human-like psychology. Specifically, our benchmark constructs 11 diverse human characters grounded in orthogonal Big Five personality traits, with each profile deeply integrated with 1,000 structured autobiographical-style episodic memories distributed across theory-grounded developmental life stages. To rigorously evaluate the psychological manifestations of LLMs, we designed a curated suite of 64 decision-making scenarios, guided by the DIAMONDS taxonomy, a psychological framework that characterizes situations along eight dimensions: Duty, Intellect, Adversity, Mating, pOsitivity, Negativity, Deception, and Sociality. By subjecting agents to varying scenarios, the benchmark evaluates whether they can consolidate their innate personality traits and autobiographical memories to make behavioral decisions that are consistent with their specific psychological profiles. After systematic human validation and filtering, we obtained a benchmark consisting of 673 multiple-choice questions (MCQs). We believe this benchmark provides a principled and scalable testbed for studying human-like emotions, personality consistency, and value-consistent behavioural decision-making in LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [197] [UniSteer: Text-Guided Flow Matching in Activation Space for Versatile LLM Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30076)
*Yingdong Shi,Ruiming Zhang,Changming Li,Zhiyu Yang,Kaixing Zhang,Jingyi Yu,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniSteer：基于文本引导的激活流匹配模型，通过学习激活空间中的条件速度场，为大型语言模型提供统一的行为控制、真实性导向、细粒度概念导向和多约束指令跟随接口


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于激活控制的方法通常依赖固定的导向方向或特定任务的干预模块，难以适应细粒度概念和组合约束

Method: 提出UniSteer模型，学习从自然语言条件到残差流激活的条件分布；通过学习通用的条件速度场代替为每个目标行为单独拟合干预；在推理时执行流反演，通过部分传输源激活到潜在状态并在目标文本条件下重新生成，然后注入冻结的LLM

Result: UniSteer在三个目标LLM上的实验表明，它能够在行为控制、真实性导向、细粒度概念导向、多约束指令跟随和激活空间分类等方面提供统一接口

Conclusion: UniSteer通过学习激活空间中的条件速度场，提供了一种统一且灵活的LLM控制方法，克服了现有方法在适应细粒度概念和组合约束方面的局限性

Abstract: Activation-based control steers large language models (LLMs) by intervening on their internal representations during inference, and has emerged as an effective paradigm for controlling behaviors such as persona and style. However, existing methods often rely on fixed steering directions or task-specific intervention modules, making them difficult to adapt to fine-grained concepts and compositional constraints. We propose UniSteer, a text-guided activation flow matching model that learns a conditional distribution over residual-stream activations from natural-language conditions. Instead of fitting a separate intervention for each target behavior, UniSteer learns a universal conditional velocity field in activation space. At inference time, UniSteer performs flow inversion by partially transporting a source activation toward a latent state and regenerating it under a target textual condition before injecting it back into the frozen LLM. The same conditional model supports activation-space classification by selecting the textual label with the lowest reconstruction energy. Experiments on three target LLMs show that UniSteer provides a unified interface across behavioral control, truthfulness steering, fine-grained concept steering, multi-constraint instruction following, and activation-space classification.

</details>


### [198] [Adaptive Targeted Dynamic Chunking for Tokenization-Free Hierarchical Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30080)
*Thang Dang,Akira Nakagawa,Kenichi Kobayashi,Koichi Shirahata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ATDC（自适应目标动态分块）方法，通过课程学习动态调整字节压缩比，提升分层模型在字节级处理中的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无分词分层模型虽能避免传统LLMs的预处理问题（如词汇表设计复杂、OOV错误、语言特定限制），但字节级方法面临压缩比优化的关键挑战，这直接影响模型处理字节数据块的性能。

Method: 提出自适应目标动态分块（ATDC）机制，利用课程学习在训练过程中逐步调整压缩比（从低压缩到高压缩），建立目标压缩比与最内层字节数（BPIC）的关系，以追踪分块大小演变。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu 100B数据集上评估，配备ATDC的分层模型在比特每字节（BPB）性能上与字节级和词元级基线相当，训练动态更稳定，下游任务性能更优，同时保持字节级处理的鲁棒性和灵活性。

Conclusion: ATDC通过自适应压缩比控制有效解决了字节级分层模型的优化难题，在保持字节处理优势的同时实现了竞争性性能，为无分词模型的发展提供了重要技术支撑。

Abstract: Tokenization-free hierarchical models are emerging as a promising alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), addressing inherent preprocessing issues such as vocabulary design complexity, out-of-vocabulary (OOV) errors, and language-specific constraints. However, a significant challenge in these byte-level methods is the optimization of the compression ratio, a critical factor that dictates model performance for processing bytes data via chunks. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Targeted Dynamic Chunking (ATDC), a novel byte-compression control mechanism designed to enhance the effectiveness of dynamic chunking within hierarchical architectures. Our approach utilizes curriculum learning to progressively adjust the compression ratio during training, transitioning from low to high compression to stabilize the learning process. We provide an analysis establishing the relationship between the target compression ratio and Bytes-Per-Innermost-Chunk (BPIC), allowing for tracking of chunk-size evolution throughout the training phase. Evaluations conducted on the FineWeb-Edu 100B dataset demonstrate that hierarchical models equipped with ATDC achieve competitive Bits-Per-Byte (BPB) performance compared to conventional baselines operating at both byte and token levels. Furthermore, the proposed method exhibits more stable training dynamics and superior final performance across diverse downstream tasks compared to models using fixed compression ratios, while maintaining the inherent robustness and flexibility of byte-level processing.

</details>


### [199] [DirectorBench: Diagnosing Long-Form Video Generation with Personalized Multi-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30090)
*Jiamin Chen,Qianben Chen,Jiawen Zhang,Yidi Wu,Yuchen Li,Xiaokun Zhang,Wangchunshu Zhou,Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DirectorBench：一个用于长视频生成的个性化多智能体诊断基准，它通过结构化元数据、用户档案和多维度评估来诊断工作流程瓶颈，而不是依赖单一的聚合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长视频生成评估基准主要关注局部视觉质量、短期时序一致性或通用提示对齐，缺乏对工作流程故障和用户依赖性偏好的诊断。随着长视频生成从单场景合成转向具有叙事结构、电影控制、音频和跨模态同步的分钟级多镜头创建，需要更精细的诊断评估方法。

Method: 提出了DirectorBench基准，它基于80个结构化元数据条目、7个用户档案和40个检查点标准，在5个维度（脚本、视觉、音频、跨模态、稳定性）上评估生成的视频。该基准支持检查点级瓶颈定位和基于用户档案的评估。

Result: 评估了4个长视频生成工作流程、6个基础LLM和7个用户档案。结果显示工作流程存在"单元间瓶颈"：转换质量平均仅为0.256，最佳工作流程为0.356，而提示级用户需求满足度平均为0.71。人工评估验证了DirectorBench与人类判断的一致性，能够捕捉人类可感知的质量差异，并揭示被聚合评分隐藏的工作流程和档案相关故障模式。

Conclusion: 诊断性和基于用户档案的基准测试对于长视频生成至关重要，DirectorBench能够识别工作流程瓶颈并提供个性化的质量评估，为改进长视频生成系统提供了有价值的诊断工具。

Abstract: Long-form video generation is rapidly moving from short, single-scene synthesis toward minute-long, multi-shot creation with narrative structure, cinematic control, audio, and cross-modal synchronization. However, evaluating such videos remains challenging, since existing benchmarks largely focus on local visual quality, short-horizon temporal consistency, or generic prompt alignment, and provide limited diagnosis of workflow failures and user-dependent preferences. We introduce DirectorBench, a personalized multi-agent diagnostic benchmark for long-form video generation. DirectorBench evaluates generated videos with respect to 80 structured metadata entries, 7 user profiles, and 40 checkpoint criteria across 5 dimensions: script, visual, audio, cross-modal, and stability. Instead of reducing quality to a single aggregate score, DirectorBench localizes checkpoint-level bottlenecks and supports profile-aware evaluation. We evaluate 4 long-form video generation workflows, 6 base LLMs, and 7 user profiles. Across workflows, DirectorBench reveals a between-unit bottleneck: transition quality averages only 0.256 and reaches 0.356 for the best workflow, while prompt-level user demand fulfillment averages 0.71. We further conduct human evaluation with 14 annotators to validate the alignment between DirectorBench and human judgment. The results show that DirectorBench captures human-perceptible quality differences and reveals workflow- and profile-dependent failure modes that are hidden by aggregate scoring. These findings highlight the importance of diagnostic and profile-aware benchmarking for long-form video generation.

</details>


### [200] [Dial HEALTHDIAL for Advice: A Multilingual and Multi-Parallel Spoken Dialogue Dataset for Knowledge-Grounded Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30107)
*Songbo Hu,Yinhong Liu,Ej Zhou,Evgeniia Razumovskaia,Xiaobin Wang,Alexander Fraser,Ivan Vulić,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HEALTHDIAL：一个大规模、多语言、多并行检索增强生成口语对话数据集，基于WHO可信内容构建，覆盖阿拉伯语、中文、英语、西班牙语四种语言，包含6000个信息寻求对话和163小时用户语音数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建口语对话数据集在方法论上具有挑战性，特别是在构建大规模、多语言、多并行数据集时。当前缺乏用于开发和评估检索增强生成(RAG)型口语对话系统的大规模多语言数据集。

Method: 基于WHO可信内容构建数据集，收集四种WHO官方语言（阿拉伯语、中文、英语、西班牙语）的6000个信息寻求对话（每种语言1500个），从母语者的多种方言中录制163小时用户语音，并标注人口统计学和社会语言学变量。

Result: 发布了包含6000个对话的HEALTHDIAL数据集，揭示了即使在资源丰富的语言间也存在一致的性能差异。提供了基准测试结果、原型系统和数据收集与系统评估工具包。

Conclusion: HEALTHDIAL为开发多语言口语对话系统提供了重要资源，揭示了跨语言性能差异，为未来研究提供了数据集、原型系统和评估工具包。

Abstract: Creating spoken dialogue datasets is methodologically challenging, and these challenges are amplified when the goal is to build multilingual, multi-parallel datasets at scale. This work introduces HEALTHDIAL, a large-scale, multilingual, and multi-parallel dataset for developing and evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based spoken dialogue systems. The dataset comprises 6,000 information-seeking dialogues (1,500 per language) grounded in trusted content from the World Health Organization (WHO) and 163 hours of user speech recorded from native speakers of diverse dialects across four official WHO languages: Arabic, Chinese, English, and Spanish. Each speaker is annotated with demographic (e.g., gender, age) and sociolinguistic (e.g., primary language, region of origin) variables. We report benchmark results across key dialogue tasks, which reveal consistent performance disparities across languages, even among high-resource ones. To support future research, we release the dataset, a prototype system, and a toolkit for data collection and system evaluation.

</details>


### [201] [CCS: Clinical Consensus Selection for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30131)
*Xi Zhang,Yingshu Li,Zaiqiao Meng,Jake Lever,Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS)框架，在推理时通过采样多个候选报告并选择具有最高临床共识的报告，改善放射学报告生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前放射学报告生成通常被构建为单路径生成任务，虽然数据规模、模型能力和检索机制的扩展推动了进展，但在推理时改善报告质量的方法仍未被充分探索。研究发现固定的放射学MLLM在其候选池中往往能生成临床质量更好的报告，这表明推理时决策是一个被忽视的瓶颈。

Method: 提出临床共识选择(CCS)框架：这是一个解码器无关的推理时选择框架，采样多个候选报告，并通过基于文本的实用性和放射学适应的多模态嵌入器计算的实用性来评估候选报告之间的共识，选择具有最高临床共识的报告。

Result: 在三个数据集和多个放射学MLLMs上，CCS一致优于单路径解码和通用的Best-of-N基线方法，在临床指标上取得了特别明显的提升。分析表明图像基础的实用性形成了与文本共识不同的选择轴，且推理时仍有很大的改进空间。

Conclusion: CCS框架通过在推理时选择临床共识最高的报告，有效地改善了放射学报告生成的质量，特别是临床指标的表现。该研究揭示了推理时决策的重要性，为未来的RRG研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) is commonly formulated as a single-path generation task, where a multimodal large language model (MLLM) produces one decoded report as the final output. While recent progress has largely been driven by scaling training data, model capacity, and retrieval mechanisms, improving report quality at inference time remains underexplored. In this work, we observe that fixed radiology MLLMs often generate clinically stronger reports elsewhere in their candidate pool than the one selected by default decoding, suggesting that inference-time decision making remains an overlooked bottleneck. To address this, we propose Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS), a decoder-agnostic inference-time selection framework that samples multiple candidate reports and selects the one with the highest clinical consensus across the rollout pool. CCS unifies text-based utilities with a radiology-adapted utility computed by an image--report-trained multimodal embedder, which measures candidate agreement beyond surface-level textual similarity. Across three datasets and multiple radiology MLLMs, CCS consistently improves inference-time performance over single-path decoding and generic Best-of-N baselines, with particularly clear gains on clinical metrics. Further analysis shows that image-grounded utility forms a selection axis distinct from textual consensus and that substantial headroom remains for improving RRG at inference time.

</details>


### [202] [CorPipe at CRAC 2026: Empty Nodes and Cross-Lingual Transfer in Multilingual Coreference Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30133)
*Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CorPipe 26是CRAC 2026多语言指代消解共享任务的获胜系统，在LLM赛道领先2.8个百分点，在无约束赛道领先9.5个百分点


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRAC 2026共享任务的主要目标是比较生成式LLM与专用系统在指代消解任务上的性能，同时引入5个新数据集和2种新语言，以推动多语言指代消解技术的发展

Method: CorPipe 26是基于CorPipe 25的改进版本，引入新变体在单个模型中同时预测空节点、提及和指代链接，采用统一的端到端架构处理这些任务

Result: 系统在两个赛道均表现最佳：LLM赛道领先2.8个百分点（平均F1得分），无约束赛道领先9.5个百分点。消融实验验证了不同模型大小、空节点预测方法和跨语言零样本评估的有效性

Conclusion: CorPipe 26证明了在单个统一模型中同时处理空节点、提及检测和指代消解的有效性，为多语言指代消解提供了新的技术路径，代码和模型已开源

Abstract: We introduce CorPipe 26, our winning submission to the CRAC 2026 Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution. The fifth edition of this shared task focuses mainly on the comparison of generative LLMs and specialized systems; additionally, 5 more datasets and 2 new languages are introduced. CorPipe 26 is an improved version of CorPipe 25, with a new variant predicting empty nodes together with mentions and coreference links in a single model. Our system outperforms all other submissions in the LLM track by 2.8 percent points and all submissions in the unconstrained track by 9.5 percent points. Furthermore, we perform a series of ablation experiments with different model sizes, empty node prediction methods, and cross-lingual zero-shot evaluation. The source code and the trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/ufal/crac2026-corpipe.

</details>


### [203] [GRUFF: LLM Pronoun Fidelity, Reasoning, and Biases in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30214)
*Fabian Mewes,Anne Lauscher,Vagrant Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一个名为GRUFF的新大规模德语数据集，用于测量语言模型中的代词保真度，研究了德语中四种不同性别系统和代词类型的影响，揭示了模型在语法一致性和鲁棒性方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于代词保真度的研究主要集中在英语上，而英语语法性别有限，几乎没有性别一致系统。德语具有复杂的语法性别和丰富的性别一致系统，但缺乏相应的研究资源。需要研究语言模型在德语语境下处理代词保真度的能力，特别是涉及多种性别系统和包含性别包容性代词的情况。

Method: 作者创建了一个大规模德语数据集GRUFF，覆盖德语名词的四种不同性别一致系统（阳性、阴性、中性、xier）和四组代词。使用这个数据集评估语言模型在德语语境下的代词保真度，包括在有干扰实体存在时的鲁棒性表现，并分析不同模型架构之间的差异。

Result: 研究发现：1）LLMs在缺乏明确上下文时对阳性和阴性实体表现出强烈的语法一致性，但对新代词xier和en则不然；2）模型通常对干扰物不具鲁棒性，但编码器模型在德语中比在英语中更鲁棒，反映了语法性别的重要性；3）职业刻板印象在不同语法格之间以及大多数模型之间相关性较差，只有架构相近的模型表现出相似的模式。

Conclusion: 德语复杂的语法性别系统显著影响语言模型的代词保真度表现，特别是在处理性别包容性代词时存在挑战。GRUFF数据集的发布为研究德语性别包容性语言和指代推理提供了重要资源，强调了在非英语语言中评估语言模型偏差和推理能力的重要性。

Abstract: Third-person singular pronouns have long been used to study stereotypical biases in language models and to test their abilities to reason about reference. More recently, the interplay between reasoning and bias has been investigated with the task of pronoun fidelity, which assesses models' abilities to correctly reuse a previously-specified pronoun for a discourse entity, independent of other potentially distracting discourse entities mentioned in between. However, such research focuses on English, which is a language with limited grammatical gender and almost no gender agreement. In this paper we contribute a novel, large-scale dataset, GRUFF, to measure pronoun fidelity in German, covering four different gender agreement systems in nouns, and four sets of pronouns. With this dataset, we show that LLMs show strong grammatical agreement for masculine and feminine entities in the absence of explicit context, but not for neopronouns xier and en. Models are generally not robust to distractors, but encoder-only models are more robust in German than in English, reflecting the importance of grammatical gender. Finally, we show that occupational stereotypes in this context are poorly correlated across grammatical cases, and across most models, except ones with closely related architectures. We release all code and data to encourage further work on gender-inclusive language and referential reasoning in German.

</details>


### [204] [Do Language Models Track Entities Across State Changes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30233)
*Zilu Tang,Qiao Zhao,Gabriel Franco,Derry Wijaya,Aaron Mueller,Sebastian Schuster,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究发现，语言模型在解决实体追踪任务时并非采用增量式追踪策略，而是使用非序列化的并行信息聚合机制，特别是在处理状态变更操作时表现出脆弱的全局抑制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注语言模型如何在没有状态变化的简单场景中解决实体绑定问题，但对于真实复杂场景下语言模型如何处理自然语言表达的多状态变更实体追踪任务缺乏深入理解。

Method: 通过行为分析和机制分析相结合的方法，研究语言模型在复杂多状态变更操作（PUT、REMOVE、MOVE）场景下的实体追踪机制，特别关注模型是否采用增量式状态追踪策略。

Result: 研究发现：1）语言模型不通过token或层级的增量式追踪策略，而是在查询出现时并行聚合相关信息；2）REMOVE操作采用脆弱的全局抑制标记机制，这预测了多种失败模式；3）通过机制分析提出了消除该抑制标记的解决方案。

Conclusion: 语言模型使用非序列化策略解决本质上的序列化任务，揭示了行为分析与机制分析相互促进的研究范式：行为结果指导机制假设，机制洞察帮助构建更全面的行为评估方法。

Abstract: Entity tracking (ET), the ability to keep track of states, is a fundamental skill that underlies complex reasoning. An increasing amount of work investigates how transformer language models (LMs) solve entity binding $\textit{without}$ state changes. However, there is limited understanding of how non-toy LMs address ET problems of realistic difficulties expressed in natural language. To this end, we investigate the mechanisms underlying ET in more complex scenarios featuring multiple state-changing operations. We find that LMs do not incrementally track world states across tokens or query-relevant states across layers, but simply aggregate relevant information in parallel at the last token when the query becomes evident. We further investigate mechanisms of individual operations ($\texttt{PUT}$, $\texttt{REMOVE}$, $\texttt{MOVE}$) to characterize this non-incremental ET mechanism. Surprisingly, LMs implement the $\texttt{REMOVE}$ operation with a fragile global suppression tag; this global removal mechanism predicts various failure modes that we confirm behaviorally. We provide a mechanistic solution of nullifying this tag to partially address this issue. Overall, our findings reveal that LMs solve a fundamentally sequential task using a non-sequential strategy. More broadly, our work illustrates how behavioral and mechanistic analyses can fruitfully interact. Behavioral results inform mechanistic hypotheses, and insights from mechanistic analyses help build stronger behavioral evaluations by predicting failure modes missing from existing evaluations.

</details>


### [205] [Knowing What to Solve Before How: Preplan Empowered LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30245)
*Shaojie Wang,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PPC框架在传统问题→计划→推理范式前增加了预计划阶段，通过明确的问题理解来提升大型语言模型在数学推理任务中的性能，在40个指标中的39个达到最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于计划的推理方法（问题→计划→推理范式）存在范式层面的缺陷：计划阶段和执行阶段都关注如何解决问题，而问题理解（识别问题类型、适用工具、可预见的陷阱）则是隐含的。为了弥补这一差距，需要明确的问题理解阶段。

Method: 提出PPC（预计划-计划-推理）框架，引入明确的问题理解阶段（预计划），形成新的问题→预计划→计划→推理范式。实现这一范式需要在两个层面确保预计划的概念完整性：1）设计三阶段合成流程，使用剧透评分检测器过滤泄漏和剧透故障，构建干净的预计划监督；2）使用复合GRPO奖励来确保生成的计划真正遵循预计划。

Result: 在四个基础模型和五个数学推理基准测试中，PPC在40个指标中的39个达到最佳结果。在maj@16和pass@16指标上分别比最强基线提高了+2.23和+3.06，且没有引入额外的推理token开销。

Conclusion: PPC框架通过引入明确的问题理解阶段（预计划）有效弥补了传统基于计划推理方法的范式级缺陷，显著提升了大型语言模型在数学推理任务中的性能，证明了对问题类型的明确识别是提升推理效果的关键。

Abstract: Current plan-based reasoning methods improve large language models (LLMs) by inserting a planning stage before execution, giving rise to the question $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. While effective, a closer examination reveals an inherent paradigm-level gap: both the planning and its execution stages decide how to solve a problem, while the prior question of what to solve; recognizing the problem type, the applicable tools, and the foreseeable pitfalls; remains entirely implicit. To bridge this gap, we propose PPC (Preplan-Plan-CoT), a framework that introduces an explicit problem-understanding stage, the preplan, yielding a new question $\rightarrow$ preplan $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. Realizing this paradigm requires safeguarding the conceptual integrity of preplan at both ends. Specifically, we design a three-stage synthesis pipeline with a spoiler-score detector that filters out leakage and spoiler failures to build clean preplan supervision, and a composite GRPO reward enforces that the generated plan genuinely follows from the preplan. Experiments across four backbones and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that PPC achieves the best results on 39 of 40 metrics, improving maj@16 and pass@16 by +2.23 and +3.06 over the strongest baseline without introducing additional inference token overhead.

</details>


### [206] [Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30251)
*Zizhuo Lin,Quanling Liu,Jinsheng Quan,Chao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Xing Shi,Jingtao Xu,Zhihui Li,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CCOPD（Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation）方法通过师生蒸馏框架，减少LLMs在多轮对话中由于逐步信息接收导致的"自我锚定漂移"问题，显著提升分片对话性能同时保持完整上下文能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在单次提示中接收完整指令时能成功解决问题，但相同信息分阶段在多轮对话中呈现时却容易失败。即使完整提示(FULL)和分片对话(RAW-SHARDED)包含相同的完整用户证据，模型仍会产生不同答案。主要原因是"自我锚定漂移"：基于部分信息产生的响应会引入未经支持的假设，这些假设后续会扭曲最终答案。

Method: 提出Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation（CCOPD）方法：使用同一基础模型扮演两个角色：1）冻结教师模型：基于干净的完整提示(FULL)进行条件化；2）可训练学生模型：通过多轮对话逐步接收相同证据。CCOPD将学生模型在其自身轨迹上的行为与教师在完整上下文中的规范行为对齐。仅在数学问题对话上进行训练。

Result: 仅使用数学问题对话训练后，CCOPD在数学和其他五个零样本领域外任务家族上，相比原始基础模型在RAW-SHARDED性能上实现了32%的平均相对提升，同时很大程度上保持了完整上下文的性能。分析表明CCOPD加强了基于用户证据的锚定，并减少了对先前助手回合污染的敏感性。

Conclusion: CCOPD方法通过师生蒸馏框架有效缓解了LLMs在多轮对话中的自我锚定漂移问题，证明了将模型在逐步信息接收场景下的行为与其在完整上下文中的规范行为对齐的有效性。该方法仅需在单一领域训练即可获得跨领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve a task when all instructions are given in a single prompt, but fail when the same information is revealed gradually across turns. When a clean FULL prompt and a RAW-SHARDED conversation contain the same complete user evidence, the model should still arrive at the same answer. We argue that a key reason for this gap is self-anchored drift: responses produced under partial information introduce unsupported assumptions, and those assumptions later distort the final answer. To reduce this effect, we propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD). During training, the same base model is used in two roles: a frozen teacher conditioned on the clean FULL prompt and a trainable student that receives the same evidence incrementally through a multi-turn conversation; CCOPD aligns the student's behavior on its own trajectories with the teacher's canonical full-context behavior. Trained only on math problem conversations, CCOPD yields a 32\% average relative improvement in RAW-SHARDED performance over the original base model across math and five zero-shot out-of-domain task families, while largely preserving full-context performance. Further analyses suggest that CCOPD strengthens grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to contamination from earlier assistant turns.

</details>


### [207] [How LoRA Remembers? A Parametric Memory Law for LLM Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30260)
*Ziwen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Linsong Yu,Benglei Cui,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过将LoRA作为潜在空间中的受控内存容量探针，系统地量化了参数化内存的精确容量限制和底层动态，提出了参数化内存定律，并基于此开发了MemFT优化策略来提升内存保真度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要在动态的真实世界环境中持续学习和更新知识以保持有效性。虽然LoRA广泛用于此类内存更新，但现有研究主要依赖定性下游评估，而对精确参数化内存的容量限制和底层动态缺乏系统的量化研究。

Method: 使用LoRA作为潜在空间中的受控内存容量探针来系统量化精确参数化内存；提出了参数化内存定律，这是一个连接损失减少ΔL与有效参数和序列长度的稳健幂律；在token级别进行细粒度分析，揭示了确定性的相变；开发了MemFT（阈值引导优化策略），动态将训练预算重新分配到低于阈值的token。

Result: 细粒度分析显示预测概率p>0.5构成了贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件；参数化内存定律得到了实证验证；MemFT通过动态调整训练预算，显著增强了内存保真度和训练效率。

Conclusion: 研究通过量化方法深入理解了参数化内存的容量和动态特性，提出的参数化内存定律为系统评估内存性能提供了理论基础，MemFT优化策略为高效内存更新提供了实用方法，有助于大语言模型在动态环境中的持续学习和知识更新。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw.

</details>


### [208] [MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30295)
*Valentina Bui Muti,Eugénie Dulout,Ziquan Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究者开发了从非结构化文本生成符合HL7 FHIR R4标准的临床数据管道，创建了MedCase-Structured数据集，发现LLMs在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确性低于纯文本输入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在临床推理和决策支持方面显示潜力，但在真实电子健康记录环境中的评估仍然有限。现有基准测试通常依赖于静态数据集或非结构化输入，不能反映临床系统中使用的结构化、可互操作数据格式。

Method: 引入了一个管道，从非结构化文本生成临床现实的HL7 FHIR R4数据包。该管道结合了分阶段LLM生成、基于术语的验证和修复，以减少幻觉代码并强制结构和语义一致性。将此方法应用于MedCaseReasoning，构建了MedCase-Structured合成数据集。

Result: 实现了82.5%案例的有效FHIR生成。在MedCase-Structured上的评估显示，LLMs在结构化FHIR输入上的诊断准确性始终低于纯文本输入。

Conclusion: 强调了对齐部署的基准测试的重要性，因为LLMs在处理真实临床结构化数据（如FHIR格式）时的性能可能低于非结构化文本处理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for clinical reasoning and decision support, but evaluation in realistic, electronic health record-congruent settings remains limited. Existing benchmarks often rely on static datasets or unstructured inputs that do not reflect the structured, interoperable data formats used in clinical systems. We introduce a pipeline for generating clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles from unstructured text, enabling controllable evaluation of clinical decision support systems. The pipeline combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to reduce hallucinated codes and enforce structural and semantic consistency. Applying this approach to MedCaseReasoning, we construct MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset aligned with clinician-authored diagnostic cases, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Evaluation on MedCase-Structured reveals consistently lower diagnostic accuracy for LLMs on structured FHIR inputs than with plain text, highlighting the importance of deployment-aligned benchmarking.

</details>


### [209] [Resolution Diagnostics for Paired LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30315)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在两大公开LLM排行榜中，大量成对排名比较在配对测试分辨率标准下无法得到可靠结论：Open LLM Leaderboard v1中有11/40，MMLU-Pro相邻排名中有4/9的对比无法在(α=0.05, 1-β=0.8)标准下得到解析。作者将配对LLM评估重构为假设检验问题，提出分辨率比q=N/N*作为主要诊断指标，发现广泛使用的非配对Cohen-h-plus-(1-ρ)简化方法在相近比较场景中偏差约2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到当前公开LLM排行榜中许多成对比较结果在统计上不可靠，特别是在相近模型比较时。现有评估方法存在统计缺陷，需要更严谨的统计框架来确保LLM评估结果的可信度。这反映了当前LLM评估领域对统计严谨性的需求，尤其是在排行榜排名可能影响模型选择和部署决策时。

Method: 1. 将配对LLM评估构建为假设检验问题
2. 提出分辨率比q=N/N*作为诊断指标，其中N为实际配对数量，N*为达到特定统计功效所需的最小配对数量
3. 推导出小效应展开的精确二阶常数
4. 分析现有常用统计工具（Cohen 1988、G*Power、R pwr）中的缺陷
5. 进行重采样分析（类别自举）和聚类校正
6. 考虑多重性校正和随时有效的序贯测试

Result: 1. Open LLM Leaderboard v1中40对比较有11对(27.5%)未达到(α=0.05, 1-β=0.8)的解析标准
2. MMLU-Pro前10名相邻排名中4/9(44.4%)未解析，考虑真实被试聚类后上升至6/9(66.7%)
3. 类别自举重采样中，未解析比例保持在5-6/9(55.6%-66.7%)
4. 发现广泛使用的非配对Cohen-h-plus-(1-ρ)简化方法在相近比较场景中与正确的N*偏差约2倍
5. 三个现成计算器(Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr)在用户后乘(1-ρ)时无声地继承了这一缺陷
6. 未解析模式在多重性校正和序贯测试下依然存在

Conclusion: 当前LLM排行榜中的许多成对比较在统计上不够可靠，特别是在模型性能相近时。常用的统计简化方法存在系统性偏差，可能导致对模型性能差异的错误结论。需要采用更严谨的统计框架，如本文提出的分辨率比诊断方法，来提高LLM评估的可信度。这一发现对模型选择、基准设计和排行榜解释具有重要启示。

Abstract: Across two public LLM leaderboards, many displayed pairwise rankings do not meet a conventional paired-test resolution target under the actual paired evaluation design: 11 of 40 Open LLM Leaderboard v1 pairwise comparisons and 4 of 9 MMLU-Pro top-10 adjacent-rank pairs are unresolved at (alpha, 1-beta) = (0.05, 0.8). The MMLU-Pro count rises to 6/9 under real subject-level clustering and stays at 5-6 out of 9 in 99.9% of category-bootstrap resamples. We frame paired LLM evaluation as a hypothesis-testing problem, invert level-alpha, power-(1-beta) tests, and report a per-pair resolution ratio q = N/N* as the primary diagnostic. A sharp small-effect expansion with an explicit second-order constant shows that the widely-used unpaired Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho) shortcut deviates from the correct N* by approximately a factor of two in the close-comparison regime, a deficit that three of five off-the-shelf calculators(Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr) silently inherit when the user post-multiplies their per-arm output by (1-rho). The unresolved-pair pattern remains under multiplicity correction and anytime-valid sequential testing.

</details>


### [210] [COMPOSE: Composing Future Theorems from Citations and Formal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30333)
*David Busbib,Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: COMPOSE：基于双重图（引文图和形式定理依赖图）生成数学领域未来定理声明的框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只建模科学引文背景或形式定理依赖关系中的一个来源，导致生成的未来数学声明要么缺乏形式基础，要么科学动机不足。需要同时考虑这两个约束来源。

Method: 提出COMPOSE双重图框架，将语言模型同时条件化在科学引文背景和形式定理结构上。构建了108K对科学-形式图数据集，以及47K个2024-2025年未来论文的基准。

Result: COMPOSE在检索真实未来论文方面优于强基线，在LLM评估中获得最佳整体性能，生成更具基础性和数学丰富性的输出。

Conclusion: 结合科学背景和形式结构对未来的数学声明生成有益，COMPOSE证明了这种双重方法的价值。

Abstract: A plausible future mathematical claim must satisfy two constraints: it should follow the direction of prior work and respect the formal dependencies that constrain what can validly follow. Existing approaches typically model only one of these sources, producing claims that are either weakly grounded or insufficiently motivated. We introduce grounded future mathematical generation, where the goal is to generate a plausible future theorem-like claim for an anchor paper using two complementary sources of context: its scientific citation graph and aligned formal theorem dependency graph. To address this setting, we propose COMPOSE, a dual-graph framework that conditions a language model on both scientific citation context and formal theorem structure. To support this setting, we construct a dataset of 108K paired scientific-formal graph examples from arXiv and Mathlib, together with a benchmark of 47K future papers from 2024--2025. Experiments show that COMPOSE outperforms strong baselines on retrieval to real future papers and achieves the best overall performance under LLM-judge evaluation, producing more grounded and mathematically richer outputs. These results show that future mathematical generation benefits from combining scientific context with formal structure. Project page is available at https://david-busbib.github.io/COMPOSE-page/.

</details>


### [211] [Unlocking the Working Memory of Large Language Models for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30343)
*Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RiM方法通过引入固定长度的记忆块代替自回归生成推理步骤，实现单前向传播的潜在推理，提升语言模型推理效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型通过生成中间推理步骤来提升推理能力，但这将内部计算与外部通信耦合。相比之下，人类认知可以利用工作记忆在内部持有和处理信息而无需外部化中间思考。受此启发，需要开发一种更高效的潜在推理方法

Method: 引入RiM方法，用固定序列的特殊令牌记忆块代替自回归生成推理步骤，解锁语言模型的工作记忆能力。采用两阶段课程训练：第一阶段通过预测显式推理步骤来基础化记忆块；第二阶段抛弃步骤级监督，在每个记忆块后迭代优化最终答案

Result: 在多种推理基准测试中，RiM方法在不同家族和尺寸的语言模型上都匹配或超越了现有潜在推理方法，同时避免了自回归生成思考过程。这表明大型语言模型可以被训练使用工作记忆作为有效的潜在推理机制

Conclusion: RiM方法通过工作记忆机制实现了高效的潜在推理，将推理能力与自回归生成解耦，展示了语言模型可以通过固定记忆块进行内部计算而不需要外部化中间步骤的可行性

Abstract: To improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models, test-time compute is typically scaled by generating intermediate tokens before the final answer. However, this couples reasoning to autoregressive generation and thereby conflates internal computation with external communication. In contrast, human cognition can use working memory to hold and manipulate information internally without the need to externalize intermediate thoughts. Drawing on this principle, we introduce Reasoning in Memory (RiM), a latent reasoning method that replaces the autoregressive generation of reasoning steps with memory blocks. These memory blocks are fixed sequences of special tokens that unlock the working-memory capacity of large language models. Since they are fixed rather than generated, they can be processed in a single forward pass, enabling compute-efficient latent reasoning. To operationalize these memory blocks, we employ a two-stage curriculum. First, we ground them by predicting explicit reasoning steps after each memory block. Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.

</details>


### [212] [LLMSurgeon: Diagnosing Data Mixture of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30348)
*Yaxin Luo,Jiacheng Cui,Xiaohan Zhao,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Liu,Xinyue Bi,Zhaoyi Li,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LLMSurgeon框架，通过生成文本后验估计LLM预训练数据混合分布，无需访问原始训练数据即可审计模型的"数字DNA"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）的预训练数据混合构成其"数字DNA"，影响模型行为、能力和失败模式，但数据组成很少被披露，使得事后审计数据组合或来源变得困难。

Method: 提出数据混合手术（DMS）概念，在仅给定目标LLM生成文本的情况下，在预定义分类法下估计其预训练语料库的领域级分布。开发LLMSurgeon框架，将DMS建模为标签偏移假设下的逆问题，通过估计校准的软混淆矩阵并求解约束逆问题来校正系统性领域混淆并恢复潜在混合先验。

Result: 引入LLMScan评估套件（基于开源LLM构建，具有透明的预训练混合），在LLMScan上，LLMSurgeon在固定协议下能够高保真地恢复领域混合分布。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用的、事后审计基础模型"数字DNA"的方法，无需访问其训练数据，为模型透明度和审计提供了新工具。

Abstract: The pretraining data mixture of Large Language Models (LLMs) constitutes their "digital DNA", shaping model behaviors, capabilities, and failure modes. Yet this composition is rarely disclosed, making post-hoc auditing of data combination or provenance difficult. In this work, we formalize $\textbf{Data Mixture Surgery (DMS)}$: given only generated text from a target LLM, estimate the domain-level distribution of its pretraining corpus under a predefined taxonomy. We propose $\textbf{LLMSurgeon}$, a strong framework that casts DMS as an inverse problem under the label-shift assumption. Rather than directly aggregating classifier outputs, LLMSurgeon estimates a calibrated $\textit{soft}$ confusion matrix and solves a constrained inverse problem to correct systematic domain confusion and recover the latent mixture prior. To evaluate, we introduce $\textbf{LLMScan}$, a recipe-verifiable evaluation suite built from open-source LLMs with transparent pretraining mixtures. Across LLMScan, LLMSurgeon recovers domain mixtures with high fidelity under fixed protocols. Our work presents a practical, post-hoc approach for auditing the digital DNA of foundation models without access to their training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了STHTD-MP方法，一种行为诱导的Mirror-Prox时序差分方法，通过用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替换协方差度量来改进鞍点几何，提供更快的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Mirror-Prox TD方法通常使用特征协方差度量，而混合TD方法表明行为策略转移信息可以提供更具信息性的更新几何。需要一种更优的度量来改进梯度时序差分方法的收敛性能。

Method: 提出了STHTD-MP方法，在原始-对偶鞍点公式中用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替换协方差度量。该方法保持原始变量和辅助变量的单一学习率，并对得到的混合鞍点算子应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤。

Result: 在标准随机逼近假设下提供了收敛性分析：行为诱导度量是正定的，联合平均系统是Hurwitz的，有界性来自Lyapunov论证。推导了投影oracle遍历间隙界，并通过确定Mirror-Prox误差矩阵的谱半径与GTD2-MP进行了精确的平均算子比较。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP相比GTD2-MP可以有更小的平均收缩因子，当行为诱导度量改进鞍点几何时。在两状态、随机游走和Boyan链基准上的数值分析支持这一条件，而Baird反例被识别为严格假设失效的奇异边界情况。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [214] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 认知范畴变换器(CCT)是一个306M参数的架构，通过集成范畴论和认知科学的组件增强预训练GPT-2 Small，在WikiText-103上相比基线降低2.92 PPL(12%相对改进)，其中84%的改进归因于GT-Full单纯消息传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在探索如何将范畴论和认知科学的原理整合到语言模型中，以改进其性能。传统语言模型主要基于统计模式，而作者试图引入更深层次的结构认知原理，验证这些认知启发的组件能否在标准语言建模任务上带来可衡量的改进。

Method: 提出了认知范畴变换器(CCT)架构，以预训练的GPT-2 Small为基础，添加了基于范畴论的认知组件。采用匹配步数协议(215,000优化器步骤)在WikiText-103上进行实验。通过消融研究验证了GT-Full单纯消息传递组件的有效性，并测试了三种一致性风格的范畴先验(层平滑、伴随往返、曲率正则化)。

Result: CCT达到21.27验证困惑度，相比基线GPT-2 Small的24.19降低了2.92 PPL(12%相对改进)。消融实验显示，GT-Full单纯消息传递贡献了84%的架构改进(2.45 of 2.92 PPL)。同时发现了"结构/一致性区分"的实证模式：添加新拓扑的范畴先验能改进语言建模，而强制一致性恒等式的则不能。

Conclusion: 研究首次提供了消融验证的证据，表明单纯消息传递在306M参数规模上能改进WikiText-103的语言模型困惑度。更重要的是，发现了范畴论先验在语言建模中的差异化效果：增加拓扑结构的组件有效，而强制一致性的组件无效，这为认知启发的语言模型设计提供了新的指导原则。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [215] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM生成的学术论文评审与人类评审对齐有限，使用迭代式游戏化策略能显著提高论文评分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的论文评审在学术界日益普及，不仅审稿人使用LLM辅助，作者也使用LLM修改论文。需要从作者和审稿人双重角度评估LLM评审的质量和影响。

Method: 基于2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文进行实证实验，评估LLM评审质量，并研究作者使用"游戏化"策略（迭代式草稿-修订工作流）的效果。

Result: 1) LLM评审与人类评审对齐有限，最佳情况下对齐程度合理；2) LLM-人类对齐在不同提示和模型间差异显著；3) 作者使用迭代式修订策略能有效提高评审分数，使35%的论文获得统计显著的评分提升。

Conclusion: LLM生成的评审存在局限性，且容易受到"游戏化"策略的影响，这对学术评审系统的完整性构成挑战，需要建立相应的防护措施和标准。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [216] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OCE是一种基于几何视角的乘法参数更新方法，通过层间正交变换实现精确的概念擦除，同时保持神经元的幅度和角度几何结构，解决了现有方法在概念擦除精度和生成能力保留之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法存在显著局限性：基于训练的方法计算成本高、可扩展性差；基于编辑的方法虽然更高效，但依赖加性参数更新，导致概念擦除精度和整体生成能力无法同时优化，因为加性更新会纠缠神经元方向、幅度和角度几何结构。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法：1）从几何视角重新形式化基于编辑的擦除为乘法参数更新；2）应用层间正交变换，该变换通过闭式解得到，可精确擦除概念同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何；3）针对多概念擦除的冲突约束，引入子空间级目标并进行结构化子空间操作。

Result: 广泛实验表明OCE在单概念和多概念擦除中都优于现有方法，在概念擦除和非目标保护方面表现优异，能够在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念，实现了精确擦除与生成能力保留的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: OCE通过几何视角的乘法参数更新方法解决了概念擦除中精度与生成能力保留的核心矛盾，为扩散模型的安全内容控制提供了更有效、可扩展的解决方案，特别在多概念擦除场景下表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [217] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFEAgent是一个基于多智能体的端到端系统，能够直接从输入图像和问题描述自动化FEA建模与仿真，通过结合视觉-语言多模态处理与验证优先的代码生成，显著提高了FEA工作流的自动化可靠性和正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析(FEA)是现代工程设计的基础，但其工作流复杂且高度依赖领域专业知识。尽管已有研究将大型语言模型(LLM)集成到FEA中，但现有方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限。

Method: 提出了VFEAgent系统，包含两个核心组件：1) 基于ReAct推理的多模态视觉-语言多智能体流水线，用于从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规范；2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，包含鲁棒的自调试和回退机制以确保可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景下的系统评估表明，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面实现了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性方面超越了基于LLM的基准方法。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了自动化完整FEA工作流的可行性，凸显了该框架解放工程师于繁琐手工分析的潜力，为工程分析的智能化自动化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [218] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议开发AI建模与仿真工具的负责任评估框架，通过自动化测试评估AI工具在因果推理、模型构建等方面的能力，发现AI工具在讨论和定性任务上表现优于因果推理和定量修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持真实世界决策的AI工具需要建立可解释的仿真模型，但当前自动化建模工具需要补充而非取代人类专业知识。需要建立负责任、伦理的人类中心建模与仿真实践基准。

Method: BEAMS倡议使用开放数字和组织基础设施协作评估AI建模与仿真工具。通过开源sd-ai项目确保透明度，由指导组确定基准优先级，技术组实现自动化测试。测试包括因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建议建模步骤和修正建议等类别。

Result: 评估显示：1) AI建模工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现更好；2) 在因果推理和定量错误修正方面较弱；3) 不同LLM在不同引擎类型上没有明显主导优势，特定任务存在速度与准确度的权衡；4) 性能在不同AI工具间存在变异性。

Conclusion: BEAMS倡议建立了AI建模工具的评估框架，证明了AI工具在定性任务上的优势和在因果推理方面的局限。未来工作将纳入考虑偏见和人类中心用例的基准，推动负责任AI建模工具的发展。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [219] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现用户与大型语言模型的互动习惯具有高度的稳定性，个体用户行为随时间变化很小，用户异质性显著。Copilot数据显示群体层面有明显趋势，但个体层面变化微弱。WildChat数据集偏向高熟练度"专家用户"，不能代表典型的用户-AI互动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要描述用户与LLM的静态互动，缺乏对个体用户行为随时间变化的动态理解。需要研究用户如何随时间调整与LLM的互动习惯，以及不同活跃度用户之间的差异。

Method: 分析了约12,000名随机抽样的Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行比较。研究了用户行为的动态变化和不同活跃度用户群体的差异。

Result: 1. Copilot数据显示显著的群体层面趋势，但个体用户轨迹的趋势要弱得多，用户习惯具有高度的"粘性"；2. 不同活跃度用户存在明显差异：更活跃的用户对话更成功，使用LLM处理更复杂和专业导向的任务；3. WildChat-4.8M数据集存在显著偏向，主要反映高熟练度"专家用户"的行为模式。

Conclusion: 现有用户行为难以改变，用户异质性显著。WildChat数据集不能代表典型的用户-AI互动，这对该数据集的下游使用具有重要警示意义。研究强调需要理解个体用户行为的稳定性和群体内部的多样性。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [220] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个解释性审计管道，用于评估大型语言模型在公共评论分类中的分歧，发现模型间的解释性差异超过模型内提示变化，需要将分歧作为准确性指标的补充评估方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构使用大型语言模型对公共评论语料库进行分类，模型的分类方式直接影响政策制定者看到的内容和哪些论点被采纳。传统的基于少量验证集立场准确性的评估方法无法检测不同模型对相同公共输入产生的实质性不同分类

Method: 提出解释性审计管道，将多模型分歧视为解释复杂性的诊断指标，并将人工审查引向真正模糊的公共输入。分析了美国农业部联邦记录中的1,260条公共评论，使用四个LLM进行比较；进行了两阶段标注研究，四个LLM和人类标注员独立标注并在看到他人标注后修订
